| 0016 | EXISTING | animation | `animation.add_layer_function` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:976) | Declare a new layer function on an anim layer interface BP (BPTYPE_Interface UAnimBlueprint). Spawns an AnimationGraphSchema-bound function graph + UAnimGraphNode_Root output pose node. Optional `pins` declares linked input pose/function pins by creating/updating UAnimGraphNode_LinkedInputPose: e.g. [{name:'SourcePose',type:'pose'},{name:'AimYaw',type:'real'}]. `connect_first_pose_to_output` defaults true and pass-throughs the first pose into the function output when possible. Idempotent: returns {already:true} if function already declared. Scans other project/game-feature ALIs and reports same-named layer collisions as collision_warnings[] without blocking creation. Returns {function_name, graph_name, root_node_id, schema, linked_input_pose_readback?, collision_warnings, collision_warning_count, compiled}. |
| 0017 | EXISTING | animation | `animation.add_layer_function_override` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:986) | Spawn an AnimationGraphSchema-bound override graph for a single anim layer interface function on a child AnimBP. Equivalent to UE Editor 'Implement Function' on My Blueprint > Interfaces > <ALI> > <function>. `path` is the child AnimBP, `interface_path` is the ALI, `function_name` is one declared layer function. Auto-creates UAnimGraphNode_Root output pose node so the graph compiles immediately. Idempotent: returns {already:true} if override graph exists. -32602 if interface not implemented on child or function not declared on interface. Returns {function_name, graph_name, schema, output_node_id, compiled}. |
| 0018 | EXISTING | animation | `animation.add_layered_blend_per_bone` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:751) | Spawn a UAnimGraphNode_LayeredBoneBlend (upper/lower body split, etc.). | 0019 | EXISTING | animation | `animation.add_linked_anim_layer_node` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:996) | Spawn a UAnimGraphNode_LinkedAnimLayer node in a master AnimBP's AnimGraph (replaces the bare placeholder animation.add_link_anim_layer). Sets Interface (UClass) + Layer (FName, the interface function) before ReconstructNode so the engine wires InputPose/OutputPose pins automatically. Scalar ALI input parameters are exposed as custom property pins by default (`expose_input_properties`: true) so bind_anim_node_property can bind call-site params like AimYaw/AimPitch. Pose pins return disconnected; use animation.connect_pose_pin to wire into the master AnimGraph. Master BP must implement `interface_path`; otherwise -32602. `instance_class_path` is optional (forces a specific child class at design-time; runtime can override via Mesh->LinkAnimClassLayers). Returns {node_id, class, interface, interface_function, instance_class, pin_count, input_pose_pin, output_pose_pin, input_property_pins, pins, compiled}. |

For each detected node, fires PostEditChangeProperty(Layer/Interface) + ReconstructNode to apply the canonical reconstruction pipeline. `path` optional (single AnimBP scope; if omitted scans every project/game-feature AnimBP via AssetRegistry + TObjectIterator). `dry_run` (default false) reports detected nodes without modifying. `compile` (default false) compiles every changed BP after repair. | 0198 | EXISTING | asset | `asset.migrate_from_project` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:2659) | Migrate assets from an external Unreal project into the active project by launching the source project with UnrealEditor-Cmd and invoking the source editor's AssetTools.migrate_packages API. By default computes dependency closure in the source AssetRegistry, but `include_dependencies:false` migrates only the selected seed packages. Dependency closure can be narrowed with class/path allow/deny lists, and dry_run returns selected_assets plus skipped_dependencies with reasons before any write. Accepts explicit source_assets or source_folder/source_folders and expands folders through the source AssetRegistry. Reports destination conflicts, rejects non-dry writes unless overwrite=true, verifies destination package files after migration, and treats non-zero commandlet exits as failures unless writes were independently verified. Subfolder destinations are handled by migrating to the resolved Content root and then relocating the staged assets in the target editor with Unreal asset rename APIs; responses include commandlet_log_path and commandlet_log_tail for diagnosis. The commandlet passes MigrationOptions with ignore dependencies enabled by default so the final copy set is the filtered selected set; actual disk writes are snapshot/diff checked, reported as actual_written_assets/ extra_written_assets/denied_written_assets, and any unexpected write rejects the migration with rollback diagnostics. If AssetTools skips selected redirector/ alias packages but writes their real target packages, the target editor relocates actual_written_assets into the requested destination package root and reports the skipped aliases as missing_selected_alias_assets and relocation_ignored_missing_selected_assets instead of failing the migration. After relocation, migrated UAnimationAsset packages are loaded, checked for restart-stable Skeleton references under the requested destination root, repaired with SetSkeleton when a unique migrated USkeleton candidate exists, saved, and reported through animation_skeleton_verification; this verification scans the destination package root for UAnimationAsset/USkeleton assets so pre-existing migrated skeleton dependencies are still considered. Overwrite relocation now reports relocation_overwrite_delete_reports and can clear stale or invalid target duplicates with editor delete/force-delete diagnostics before moving the valid staged package; staged redirectors are fixed up through AssetTools so referencers are rewritten to the requested destination root. `source_project` accepts a .uproject or project directory; `source_assets` are package/object paths; `destination_mount_or_path` may be /Game..., a plugin mount like /FlightCore, or a Content folder path. |

| 0479 | EXISTING | level | `level.load` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:2073) | Load a level asset (replaces persistent level). Rejects during PIE. If the current world is dirty, rejects -32602 unless `discard_unsaved:true` is set (mirrors editor's 'Save before opening?' dialog). Accepts long package paths, object paths, and .umap filenames, including mounted GameFeature/plugin maps. Uses the Python reference cleanup guard by default before loading (disable with cleanup_python_refs=false). Uses the editor map-loading API, polls until GEditor reports the requested persistent world, and falls back to opening the UWorld asset if needed; returns -32603 with method/status details if the world still does not switch. Returns {path, requested_package, loaded_package, world_path, load_map_ok, open_asset_fallback_*, poll_count, loaded}. |

| 0623 | EXISTING | project | `project.create_cpp_class` | Sage schema (server/src/tools/phase4_schemas.cpp:2364) | Create a UCLASS .h + .cpp pair in a project module. Required: class_name. Optional: parent_class (default UObject), module (default = project name), subfolder (under Source/<Module>/). Auto UHT compliance: applies conventional prefix from parent ('A' for AActor descendants, 'U' for UObject, ...) and includes the parent's engine header for ~30 common base classes (AActor, APawn, ACharacter, AGameModeBase, UActorComponent, USceneComponent, UCharacterMovementComponent, UAnimInstance, UUserWidget, etc.). For custom/unknown bases pass parent_header (engine-relative path like 'GameFramework/Character.h'). If the module's Build.cs doesn't exist, pass bootstrap_module=true to scaffold a fresh native module: writes Build.cs + module .h/.cpp + <Project>.Target.cs + <Project>Editor.Target.cs and patches the .uproject Modules[] array. Editor restart required afterward to compile (Mac: no live coding, must rebuild from terminal or via restart_editor MCP tool). |

## Implementation Order

1. AI/BT/Blackboard/EQS/StateTree/SmartObject.
2. Mesh/GeometryScript/ProceduralMesh/RealtimeMesh.
3. Audio/SoundCue/MetaSound.
4. UI/CommonUI/templates/accessibility.
5. Material instance/function/HLSL/inspection.
6. C++ API/reflection/network/risk/decision intelligence.
7. Runtime verification/capture/performance.
8. Asset/folder/DataTable/Fab/ISM/HISM.
9. Niagara remaining tranche.

## Notes

- Planned entries are reference-derived capability tokens, not yet accepted API names. During implementation, a planned action may become a Sage-style explicit tool name, a schema expansion, or a grouped helper if that preserves better ergonomics.
- Existing entries are current source truth as of this checkout and include earlier unbuilt source changes. Build verification is intentionally deferred by user instruction.
- Counts are asserted during generation; this file should not be hand-edited without rerunning the count check.
